ACTUAL TEST test_d110_controlled_success 551 689 materialise count 7 ORACLE Actual independently accepted D109 profile and current actual D109 5/1; C1 R118 111; exact 500/111 percent, not strike110 or missing factor100; ordinary read-only reopen and retry.
ACTUAL TEST test_d110_zero_spot 692 824 materialise count 6 ORACLE Actual same accepted core D109=5/1; selected actual finite C1 spot 0, correct metadata; refuse >0.
ACTUAL TEST test_d110_negative_spot 827 959 materialise count 6 ORACLE Actual same core D109=5/1, selected actual C1 spot -1 finite, correct metadata; reject nonzero negative.
ACTUAL TEST test_d110_missing_spot 962 1083 materialise count 6 ORACLE Actual D109 materialises with six core records, no R118 anywhere; D110 refuses missing spot.
ACTUAL TEST test_d110_wrong_spot_right 1086 1219 materialise count 6 ORACLE Actual D109 valid, genuine C2 R118 present, C1 absent; no opposite-leg fallback.
ACTUAL TEST test_d110_spot_original_units 1222 1352 materialise count 6 ORACLE Actual D109 valid; C1 R118 original USD_per_contract while mapping says USD_per_share; refuse.
ACTUAL TEST test_d110_spot_wrong_event 1355 1486 materialise count 6 ORACLE Actual D109 valid; C1 R118 event T-1 receipt T visible but exact T mismatch; refuse.
ACTUAL TEST test_d110_spot_ambiguous 1489 1629 materialise count 6 ORACLE Actual D109 valid; two distinct eligible C1 R118 at T, 111/112; refuse, no sequence ranking.
ACTUAL TEST test_d110_missing_parent 1632 1762 materialise count 6 ORACLE Actual legal D109 and lawful spot exist; request nonexistent D109 ID; no reconstruction or output.
ACTUAL TEST test_d110_wrong_core 1765 1903 materialise count 6 ORACLE Use actual accepted BASE D109 with C60 core and legal R118; reject incompatible core before spot arithmetic.

from decimal import Decimal
os.chdir('/home/jds/nvda_scalar_straddle_strict_tester_intake_20261126')

def ck(k,a,b):
 print(k,repr(a)[:400],'MATCH' if a==b else 'MISMATCH expected '+repr(b)[:400],flush=True);assert a==b,k
leaf=P.Path('handoff/m02_completion_28_execution/L10c')
inv=json.loads((leaf/'SCALAR_STRADDLE_FINITE_CASES_PROPOSED_20261125.json').read_bytes());rows=[r for r in inv['physical_cases'] if r['family']=='D110']
# Each directly materialises genuine D087/D091/D109 and exercises actual D110 request,
# ordinary read-only predecessor and output history. Inspect complete suffix (specific spot/request/refusal assertions).
for f,row in zip(fs,rows,strict=True):
 print('\nCASE',row['case_id'],row['reached_stage'],row['specimen'],row['oracle'],'lines',f.lineno,f.end_lineno)
 text=ast.get_source_segment(s,f)
 assert 'f.create_scalar_straddle_source(' in text and 'f.create_window_owner(' in text and 'f.create_scalar_d091_owner(' in text
 assert 'f.create_d109_scalar_owner(' in text and 'f.create_d109_scalar_reader(' in text and '_assert_straddle(straddle,' in text
 assert 'f.create_d110_scalar_owner(' in text and 'f.create_d110_scalar_reader(' in text and 'move.materialise(move_request)' in text
 assert '_spot_page(source, members, C1, c1_page)' in text and '_spot_page(source, members, C2, c2_page)' in text
 if row['case_id']!='D110-CONTROLLED-SUCCESS':
  assert 'assert move.materialise(move_request) is None' in text
  assert 'assert move.reads.list_derived_history(**_history_args("D110", PAIR)) == ()' in text
  assert 'assert reader.list_derived_history(**_history_args("D110", PAIR)) == ()' in text
 else:
  assert '_assert_move(' in text and 'False)' in text and '_assert_readback(reader,' in text
 print(text[text.index('    c1_page ='):],flush=True)
# Exact static independent ratio; no import product.
ck('authority rational arithmetic',100*Fraction(5,1)/Fraction(Decimal('111')),Fraction(500,111))
# Re-read actual support DBs directly (hash and stored row bytes), matching already accepted forensics.
E=P.Path('/home/jds/nvda_scalar_support16_20261126_jz_vjglf');d=json.loads((E/'readonly_sqlite_forensics.json').read_bytes());sha=lambda b:hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()
for db in d['databases']:
 p=E/'pytest_temp'/db['path'];before=sha(p.read_bytes());ck('original support file hash '+db['path'],before,db['sha256_before']);ck('prior forensic unchanged',before,db['sha256_after'])
 c=sqlite3.connect(p.as_uri()+'?mode=ro',uri=True)
 try:
  c.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON');ck('support integrity',c.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchall(),[('ok',)])
  rows=c.execute('select derived_occurrence_id,logical_payload,content_sha256 from mmt_record_v1 order by derived_occurrence_id').fetchall()
 finally:c.close()
 ck('actual support rows/payloads '+db['path'],rows,[(r['id'],r['logical_payload_utf8'].encode(),r['content_sha256']) for r in db['rows']])
 for ident,raw,dig in rows:ck('actual payload '+ident,sha(raw),dig)
 ck('support file unchanged after read-only',sha(p.read_bytes()),before)
ck('actual no product or pytest imports',any(n.startswith(('nvda_desk','pytest','_pytest')) for n in sys.modules),False)
print('D110_SEMANTIC_STATIC_AND_SUPPORT_DURABLE_OK',flush=True)

def ck(k,a,b):
